     142 Terraria.GameContent/PlayerEyeHelper.cs
namespace Terraria.GameContent;

public struct PlayerEyeHelper
{
	private enum EyeFrame
	{
		EyeOpen,
		EyeHalfClosed,
		EyeClosed
	}

	private enum EyeState
	{
		NormalBlinking,
		InStorm,
		InBed,
		JustTookDamage,
		IsModeratelyDamaged,
		IsBlind,
		IsTipsy,
		IsPoisoned
	}

	private EyeState _state;

	private int _timeInState;

	private const int TimeToActDamaged = 20;

	public int EyeFrameToShow { get; private set; }

	public void Update(Player player)
	{
		SetStateByPlayerInfo(player);
		UpdateEyeFrameToShow(player);
		_timeInState++;
	}

	private void UpdateEyeFrameToShow(Player player)
	{
		EyeFrame eyeFrameToShow = EyeFrame.EyeOpen;
		switch (_state)
		{
		case EyeState.NormalBlinking:
		{
			int num = _timeInState % 240 - 234;
			eyeFrameToShow = ((num >= 4) ? EyeFrame.EyeHalfClosed : ((num < 2) ? ((num >= 0) ? EyeFrame.EyeHalfClosed : EyeFrame.EyeOpen) : EyeFrame.EyeClosed));
			break;
		}
		case EyeState.InStorm:
			eyeFrameToShow = ((_timeInState % 120 - 114 < 0) ? EyeFrame.EyeHalfClosed : EyeFrame.EyeClosed);
			break;
		case EyeState.IsModeratelyDamaged:
		case EyeState.IsTipsy:
		case EyeState.IsPoisoned:
			eyeFrameToShow = ((_timeInState % 120 - 100 < 0) ? EyeFrame.EyeHalfClosed : EyeFrame.EyeClosed);
			break;
		case EyeState.InBed:
		{
			EyeFrame eyeFrame = (DoesPlayerCountAsModeratelyDamaged(player) ? EyeFrame.EyeHalfClosed : EyeFrame.EyeOpen);
			_timeInState = player.sleeping.timeSleeping;
			eyeFrameToShow = ((_timeInState >= 60) ? ((_timeInState < 120) ? EyeFrame.EyeHalfClosed : EyeFrame.EyeClosed) : eyeFrame);
			break;
		}
		case EyeState.IsBlind:
			eyeFrameToShow = EyeFrame.EyeClosed;
			break;
		case EyeState.JustTookDamage:
			eyeFrameToShow = EyeFrame.EyeClosed;
			break;
		}
		EyeFrameToShow = (int)eyeFrameToShow;
	}

	private void SetStateByPlayerInfo(Player player)
	{
		if (player.blackout || player.blind)
		{
			SwitchToState(EyeState.IsBlind);
		}
		else
		{
			if (_state == EyeState.JustTookDamage && _timeInState < 20)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (player.sleeping.isSleeping)
			{
				bool resetStateTimerEvenIfAlreadyInState = player.itemAnimation > 0;
				SwitchToState(EyeState.InBed, resetStateTimerEvenIfAlreadyInState);
				return;
			}
			if (DoesPlayerCountAsModeratelyDamaged(player))
			{
				SwitchToState(EyeState.IsModeratelyDamaged);
				return;
			}
			if (player.tipsy)
			{
				SwitchToState(EyeState.IsTipsy);
				return;
			}
			if (player.poisoned || player.venom || player.starving)
			{
				SwitchToState(EyeState.IsPoisoned);
				return;
			}
			bool flag = player.ZoneSandstorm || (player.ZoneSnow && Main.IsItRaining);
			if (player.behindBackWall)
			{
				flag = false;
			}
			if (flag)
			{
				SwitchToState(EyeState.InStorm);
			}
			else
			{
				SwitchToState(EyeState.NormalBlinking);
			}

PlayerEyeHelper: blink state machine. `_timeInState % 240 - 234`: num in [-234, 5]. num>=4 → half-closed; num in [2,3] → closed; num in [0,1] → half-closed; else open. So every 240 ticks: at t%240 in [234,235] → half closed... wait num = _timeInState % 240 - 234; num >= 4 means _timeInState%240 >= 238 → half closed; num in [2,3] → 236-237 → closed; num in [0,1] → 234-235 → half; else open. So blink = 6 ticks every 240 (half 234-235, closed 236-237, half 238-239). EyeFrameToShow: 0=open, 1=half, 2=closed.

155		public void StartSleeping(Player player, int x, int y)
156		{
157			GetSleepingTargetInfo(x, y, out var targetDirection, out var anchorPosition, out var visualoffset);
158			Vector2 offset = anchorPosition - player.Bottom;
159			bool flag = player.CanSnapToPosition(offset);

161			{

163			}

166				return;
167			}

179			}
180			player.Bottom = anchorPosition;
181			player.ChangeDir(targetDirection);
182			Main.sleepingManager.AddPlayerAndGetItsStackedIndexInCoords(player.whoAmI, new Point(x, y), out sleepingIndex);
183			player.velocity = Vector2.Zero;

186			visualOffsetOfBedBase = visualoffset;

193		public static bool GetSleepingTargetInfo(int x, int y, out int targetDirection, out Vector2 anchorPosition, out Vector2 visualoffset)
194		{
195			Tile tileSafely = Framing.GetTileSafely(x, y);
196			if (!TileID.Sets.CanBeSleptIn[tileSafely.type] || !tileSafely.active())
197			{
198				targetDirection = 1;
199				anchorPosition = default(Vector2);
200				visualoffset = default(Vector2);
201				return false;
202			}
203			int num = y;
204			int num2 = x - tileSafely.frameX % 72 / 18;
205			if (tileSafely.frameY % 36 != 0)
206			{
207				num--;
208			}
209			targetDirection = 1;
210			int num3 = tileSafely.frameX / 72;
211			int num4 = num2;

213			{
214			case 0:
215				targetDirection = -1;
216				num4++;
217				break;
218			case 1:
219				num4 += 2;
220				break;
221			}
222			anchorPosition = new Point(num4, num + 1).ToWorldCoordinates(8f, 16f);
223			visualoffset = SetOffsetbyBed(tileSafely.frameY / 36);
224			return true;
225		}

131	  if (id === undefined) { id = ++tintImgSeq; tintImgId.set(img as object, id); }
132	  const key = `t${id}|` + colorKey(color);
133	  let c = tintCache.get(key);
134	  if (c) return c;
135	  if (tintCache.size >= TINT_CACHE_MAX) {  // 满即清(防无界)
136	    for (const old of tintCache.values()) { old.width = 0; old.height = 0; }
137	    tintCache.clear();
138	  }
139	  c = document.createElement('canvas');
140	  c.width = img.width; c.height = img.height;
141	  const ctx = c.getContext('2d')!;
142	  ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;
143	  ctx.drawImage(img, 0, 0);
144	  ctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'multiply';
145	  ctx.fillStyle = `rgb(${color.r},${color.g},${color.b})`;
146	  ctx.fillRect(0, 0, c.width, c.height);
147	  ctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'destination-in';
148	  ctx.drawImage(img, 0, 0);
149	  tintCache.set(key, c);
150	  return c;
151	}
152	
153	function appearanceKey(a: Appearance): string {
154	  return [
155	    a.skinVariant, a.hair,
156	    colorKey(a.skinColor), colorKey(a.undershirtColor), colorKey(a.pantsColor),
157	    colorKey(a.shoeColor), colorKey(a.shirtColor), colorKey(a.eyeColor), colorKey(a.hairColor),
158	  ].join('|');
159	}

163	  // 消费点埋点:回退 miss 时 vui 失配报告能给出"谁在查"
164	  noteVuiConsumer(`PaperDoll.sheetRect(v${variant},s${sheet})`);
165	  const r = UITextures.get(`Player_${variant}_${sheet}.png`) ?? UITextures.get(`Player_0_${sheet}.png`);
166	  return r ? (r.img as HTMLImageElement) : null;

171	 *  headArmorImage 按 后发→本体→前发→头甲 层序叠画——动态染料逐帧变色，烘焙缓存无法承载）
172	 *  opts.layer = stealth 视觉分层（PlayerDrawSet.cs:1523-1660）：'skin' = 皮肤/发/衣着
173	 *   （×s'² 消费）；'armor' = 三件甲贴图（×s' 消费，3106/蘑菇矿 B 分量 ×settled；
174	 *   'armorNoHead' 发色剂激活时头甲归叠层路径）。原版腿甲/身甲整体替代对应皮肤衣着

184	  const hit = cache.get(key);

228	    const img = sheetRect(a.skinVariant, ch.sheet);

234	    }

290	      { d: gridRect(6, 1, 1 + dy, shirt) },                                     // 后肩·衬衫

292	      { d: gridRect(6, 0, 1 + dy, shirt) },                                     // 前肩·衬衫

339	  const idx = Math.max(0, Math.min(BODY_FRAMES - 1, frame));

353	export function tintRGBA(src: HTMLCanvasElement, mul: [number, number, number]): HTMLCanvasElement {
354	  const q = mul.map((m) => Math.round(m * 128) / 128);
355	  const key = `${q[0]},${q[1]},${q[2]}`;
356	  let inner = stealthTintCache.get(src);
357	  if (!inner) { inner = new Map(); stealthTintCache.set(src, inner); }
358	  const hit = inner.get(key);
359	  if (hit) return hit;
360	  const c = document.createElement('canvas');
361	  c.width = src.width; c.height = src.height;
362	  const ctx = c.getContext('2d')!;
363	  ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;
364	  ctx.drawImage(src, 0, 0);
365	  ctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'multiply';
366	  ctx.fillStyle = `rgb(${Math.round(q[0] * 255)},${Math.round(q[1] * 255)},${Math.round(q[2] * 255)})`;
367	  ctx.fillRect(0, 0, c.width, c.height);
368	  ctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'destination-in';
369	  ctx.drawImage(src, 0, 0);
370	  ctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'source-over';
371	  inner.set(key, c);
372	  if (inner.size > 48) {   // 量化档 ≤129；stealth 连续变化时留中段档位
373	    let i = 0;
374	    for (const k of inner.keys()) { if (i++ > 24) break; inner.delete(k); }
375	  }
376	  return c;
377	}

Important discovery in UpdateDead (Player.cs:16868-16888): when dead, body parts keep PHYSICS — `headPosition += headVelocity`, `bodyPosition += bodyVelocity`, `legPosition += legVelocity`, rotations accumulate, and each part's `velocity.Y += 0.1f` (gravity) with X damping 0.99 — the body FALLS APART and the pieces tumble! This is the classic Terraria death: the player sprite splits into head/torso/legs chunks that fly apart with rotation.

And they ARE DRAWN — LegacyPlayerRenderer DrawPlayerFull: ghost → DrawGhost, else → DrawPlayer (full). So dead non-ghost players ARE drawn, with the body pieces separated (headPosition/bodyPosition/legPosition offsets applied in each layer's draw: e.g., DrawPlayer_12_Skin uses `+ drawinfo.drawPlayer.bodyPosition` and `legPosition`, DrawPlayer_21_Head uses `+ drawPlayer.headPosition`). The pieces separate at death (velocity set at kill time — search where headVelocity etc. are set on death, in Killed()... `headVelocity = new Vector2(...)`).

So our `if (p.dead) return` (not drawing) is WRONG vs vanilla: vanilla draws the falling-apart corpse (until respawn), and hardcore-after-timer → ghost sprite (4-frame animated).

Also where are the death velocities set? Search "headVelocity =" in Player.cs.

Now, singleplayer softcore death: dead=true, ghost=false → drawn as tumbling pieces + death screen overlay? Wait — but the death overlay "You were slain" covers the screen with darkness; do you still see your body? In singleplayer, yes — you can see your character's body pieces fall apart and lie there during the respawn countdown. Yes! That's right — the corpse pieces lie on the ground. ✓ Our implementation: nothing drawn. Missing entirely.

Ghost (multiplayer softcore? or hardcore): ghost drawn with Ghost.png animated. Hmm wait — for softcore MULTIPLAYER: difficulty==0 → else branch → respawn when timer hits 0; ghost never set?? But multiplayer softcore players DO become ghosts immediately on death... Actually NO — in multiplayer softcore, you also see "You were slain" and wait; the GHOST mode is only hardcore?? Hmm, no... Let me think about vanilla 1.4 multiplayer: when a softcore player dies in multiplayer, they immediately become a ghost and can fly around while waiting to respawn. YES that's true — ghosts flying around are a classic multiplayer sight... but those might be mediumcore/hardcore ghosts. Hmm, actually I believe: in multiplayer, ALL difficulties become ghosts on death (can fly, can't interact); softcore ghost auto-respawns when timer ends. For hardcore, the ghost persists until you leave (permanent death).

But the code shows ghost=true ONLY in the hardcore branch. Let me search where else ghost gets set for multiplayer — maybe in Main.cs on receiving msg about player death, or in Player.Killed? grep showed only 16902 for "ghost = true" in Player.cs. 38:function dollEquipFromInv(inv: Inventory, atlas: import('../assets/SpriteAtlas').SpriteAtlas | null): { head: number | null; body: number | null; legs: number | null } {
6818:      let dollEquip = dollEquipFromInv(p.inv, this.atlas);
8381:      const equip = dollEquipFromInv(player.inv, this.atlas) as { head?: number | null };
function dollEquipFromInv(inv: Inventory, atlas: import('../assets/SpriteAtlas').SpriteAtlas | null): { head: number | null; body: number | null; legs: number | null } {
  const idx = (itemId: number | null | undefined, slot: 0 | 1 | 2): number | null => {
    if (itemId == null) return null;
    const def = ITEM_DEFS[itemId];
    if (!def) return null;
    // vi_ 装备：stats 表部位 + 贴图序号（hs/bs/ls = 原版 headSlot/bodySlot/legSlot）
    const st = statOfInternal(itemId);
    if (st?.s) {
      const part = st.s === 'h' ? 0 : st.s === 'b' ? 1 : 2;
      if (part !== slot) return null;
      const tex = part === 0 ? st.hs : part === 1 ? st.bs : st.ls;
      return tex ?? null;
    }
    // legacy/place_ 物品：def.armor + vanilla.json armorIndex 查表
    if (!def.armor) return null;
    const key = def.key;
    const vid = VANILLA_ITEM_ICON_MAP[key] ?? (key.startsWith('vi_') ? parseInt(key.slice(3), 10) : NaN);
    if (!Number.isFinite(vid)) return null;
    const entry = atlas?.vanilla.armorIndex?.[String(vid)];
    if (!entry) return null;
    if (def.armor.slot !== slot) return null;
    return slot === 0 ? (entry.head || null) : slot === 1 ? (entry.body || null) : (entry.legs || null);
  };
  const disp = inv.displayArmor();
  return { head: idx(disp[0], 0), body: idx(disp[1], 1), legs: idx(disp[2], 2) };
}

	private void RefreshPlayerDrawOrder()
	{
		_playersThatDrawBehindNPCs.Clear();
		_playersThatDrawAfterProjectiles.Clear();
		if (gameMenu)
		{
			return;
		}
		Player player = null;

			{

## 严重（可见行为缺失）

**1. 死亡表现整体缺失（我们 `if (p.dead) return` 不画是错的）**
- 原版证据：`Player.cs:16878-16888`（UpdateDead：头/身/腿三段 `position += velocity`、`rotation += velocity.X*0.1`、`vy+=0.1`、`vx*=0.99` 持续解体翻滚）＋ `Player.cs:16873-16877`（`immuneAlpha += 2` 至 255）＋ `Player.cs:53253` GetImmuneAlpha（`(255-immuneAlpha)/255` → 尸体约 2.1s 渐隐）；初速 `Player.cs:38287-38293`（Y=Next(-40,-10)*0.1，X=Next(-20,21)*0.1+2*hitDirection；stoned 时三段归零不散 :38294-38298）；绘制不跳过：`LegacyPlayerRenderer.cs:479-482`（非 invis 一律 DrawPlayer 全纸娃娃）、`Main.cs:22118-22130`（本地玩家恒在绘制列表）；硬core 计时归零后画幽灵：`Player.cs:16902` + `LegacyPlayerRenderer.cs:273-282/515-524`（Ghost.png 4 帧，`Player.cs:6435-6450` ghostFrame 每 8t 前进 %4）。
- 我们现状：`Renderer.ts:6601` 直接 return，只有 drawDeathScreen 遮罩；无尸块、无渐隐、无幽灵。
- 修复规格：死亡时按 ：38287-38293 公式给三段初速，每 tick 按 UpdateDead 公式推进（stoned 门照抄）；渲染把纸娃娃拆头/躯干/腿三段分别带 headPosition/headRotation（或近似为整体翻滚）绘制，alpha=(255-immuneAlpha)/255；难度 2 且 respawnTimer 归零 → 画 Ghost.png 动画帧。

**2. 跳跃/下落行号用错（1/4 应为 5/6）**
- 原版证据：`Player.cs:35808-35816`（空中腿恒 legFrame=行5；翼22/28/45→行0）；`Player.cs:36121-36161`（body：无翼升降均行5；sliding→3、sandStorm/carpet→6、eocDash→6；有翼：升/按住跳降=行6、松跳降=行5、翼22/28/45=行0）。贴图实证：行5=收腿、行6=屈腿分摆，行1-4 是挥动动作行。
- 我们现状：`Renderer.ts:6844/6021` `row = p.vy < 0 ? 1 : 4`——跳跃时腿不收、姿态是“使用物品”行。

**3. 行走循环行集与速度公式偏差**
- 原版证据：`Player.cs:35829-35847`：`legFrameCounter += |velocity.X| * 1.3`，每过 8 进一行，行集 7..19 共 **13 行**回绕；走动时 body 直接=legFrame（:36184-36186）。冰面/风推无输入时腿=行0（:35819-35826）。
